&lt;/project_context>
你需要在 westd 服务器上顺序完成 3 个紧急修复任务。服务器 SSH 名称: westd。Python 路径: /root/miniconda3/bin/python。

## Fix 1: GPU 清理（最先做）

SSH 到 westd，执行以下检查和清理：

```bash
nvidia-smi
```

检查：
1. GPU 0: 如果 PID 173479 仍在跑 evaluate.py for olmo2_d005 但 .done 已存在 → kill
```bash
ls /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_v2/olmo2_7b_d005/.done 2>/dev/null && echo "DONE EXISTS"
```
如果 .done 存在，kill 僵尸进程：
```bash
kill 173479 2>/dev/null
```

2. GPU 1: 检查是否有隐藏进程（100% util 但 0 MiB 是异常）
```bash
fuser /dev/nvidia1 2>/dev/null
```

3. 确保 4 张卡全部干净空闲（nvidia-smi 验证无残留进程）

清理完成后报告每张卡的状态。

## Fix 2: phase2_summary.csv 重新生成

当前 CSV 有 7 条 MISSING，但数据现在全部齐了。需要重新扫描 eval_results_v2/ 生成完整 25-row CSV。

创建一个 Python 脚本 `/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/regen_csv.py`，功能：
- 扫描 `/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_v2/` 下所有子目录
- 每个子目录应包含 MMLU 和 ARC 的 eval 结果文件（检查 results.json 或类似输出文件）
- 提取每个 model×dosage 的 MMLU accuracy 和 ARC accuracy
- 生成完整的 25-row CSV（5 families × 5 dosages），列: family, model_name, dosage, mmlu_accuracy, arc_accuracy
- 输出到 `/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/phase2_summary.csv`

先 `ls /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_v2/` 了解目录结构，然后根据实际文件格式编写脚本。

运行脚本并验证输出有 25 行数据（不含表头）：
```bash
wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/phase2_summary.csv
```

## Fix 3: IRT fitting 重跑（25/25 数据）

之前的 fit_irt_dose_response.py 只用了 21/25 数据（缺 OLMo d000-d050）。必须重跑。

先检查脚本：
```bash
head -100 /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/fit_irt_dose_response.py
```

然后运行（加环境变量避免 HF 联网）：
```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/exp001 && HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1 /root/miniconda3/bin/python fit_irt_dose_response.py
```

**关键验证**：输出中必须显示 "25 respondents"（不是 21）。如果仍然是 21，需要检查脚本中的数据路径扫描逻辑，确保包含了 olmo2_7b 的 d000/d005/d025/d050 目录，修复后重跑。

注意：当前脚本是 2PL（无 guessing parameter）。先用 2PL 跑完整 25 数据生成初步结果即可。

完成后报告：
1. Fix 1: 每张 GPU 的清理状态
2. Fix 2: CSV 行数和内容摘要
3. Fix 3: IRT 输出是否显示 25 respondents，以及 fitting 结果摘要

Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

{
  "server": "westd-29999",
  "command": "cat << 'PYEOF' > /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/regen_csv.py\nimport json, csv, os, re\n\nBASE = \"/root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_v2\"\nOUT = os.path.join(BASE, \"..\", \"phase2_summary.csv\")\n\nrows = []\nfor d in sorted(os.listdir(BASE)):\n    full = os.path.join(BASE, d)\n    if not os.path.isdir(full):\n        continue\n    m = re.match(r\"^([a-z0-9]+)_(d\\d+)$\", d)\n    if not m:\n        continue\n    family, dosage = m.group(1), m.group(2)\n    \n    ef = os.path.join(full, \"eval_results.json\")\n    if not os.path.exists(ef):\n        print(f\"WARN: {d} missing eval_results.json\")\n        continue\n    \n    with open(ef) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    \n    bm = data.get(\"benchmarks\", {})\n    mmlu_acc = bm.get(\"mmlu\", {}).get(\"accuracy\")\n    arc_acc = bm.get(\"arc_challenge\", {}).get(\"accuracy\")\n    model_name = d\n    \n    rows.append({\n        \"family\": family,\n        \"model_name\": model_name,\n        \"dosage\": dosage,\n        \"mmlu_accuracy\": mmlu_acc,\n        \"arc_accuracy\": arc_acc,\n    })\n\nrows.sort(key=lambda r: (r[\"family\"], r[\"dosage\"]))\n\nwith open(OUT, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n    w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=[\"family\", \"model_name\", \"dosage\", \"mmlu_accuracy\", \"arc_accuracy\"])\n    w.writeheader()\n    w.writerows(rows)\n\nprint(f\"Wrote {len(rows)} rows to {OUT}\")\nfor r in rows:\n    print(f\"  {r['family']:8s} {r['dosage']:5s}  MMLU={r['mmlu_accuracy']:.4f}  ARC={r['arc_accuracy']:.4f}\")\nPYEOF\n/root/miniconda3/bin/python /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/regen_csv.py"
}
